Requirements

finance,

and

services.

Method
 
/
 
Evidence
 
Process:
 
identify
 
customer/investor
 
→
 
verify
 
account

and
 
product
 
eligibility
 
→
 
check
 
risk/suitability
 
constraints
 
→

approved
 
financial
 
data
 
→
 
present
 
permitted
 
information
 
→

required
 
approval
 
before
 
an
 
order
 
or
 
disbursement
 
→
 
retain
 
the
 
decision
 
trail.

a
 
loan-limit
 
request
 
is
 
checked
 
against
 
policy
 
and
 
routed
 
to
 
the
 
authorized
 
credit
 
workflow.

or

checks

/

and

and

disclosures;

obtain

or

No
 
autonomous
 
investment
 
order,
 
credit
 
approval,
 
disbursement
 
or
 
suitability
 
conclusion
 
unless
 
the
 
configured
 
workflow
 
expressly
 
permits
 
it;
 
no
 
guaranteed-return
 
statements;
 
escalate
 
conflicts,
 
vulnerable
 
customers
 
and
 
unusual
 
transactions.

/
 
Agent

Rules

must
 
distinguish
 
factual
 
information
 
from
 
regulated
 
advice;
 
use
 
approved
 
product
 
data;
 
avoid
 
guaranteed-return
 
or
 
approval
 
claims;
 
preserve
 
rationale
 
for
 
material
 
recommendations;
 
route
 
regulated
 
advice
 
and
 
decisions
 
to
 
qualified
 
staff
 
where
 
required.

status,

information

rules
 
→

the
 
case
 
→

disputed
 
coverage
 
or
 
high-risk
 
claims.

accident
 
call
 
can
 
open
 
a
 
claim,

is

to
 
a

specialist.

Industry-Specific
 
Requirements
 
Consumer/business
 
finance,
 
lending,
 
investment,
 
brokerage
 
and
 
financial-market
 
services.
 
Verification
 
Method
 
/
 
Evidence
 
Process:
 
identify
 
customer/investor
 
→
 
verify
 
account
 
authority
 
and
 
product
 
eligibility
 
→
 
check
 
risk/suitability
 
constraints
 
→
 
retrieve
 
approved
 
financial
 
data
 
→
 
present
 
permitted
 
information
 
→
 
obtain
 
required
 
approval
 
before
 
an
 
order
 
or
 
disbursement
 
→
 
retain
 
the
 
decision
 
trail.
 
Example:
 
a
 
loan-limit
 
request
 
is
 
checked
 
against
 
policy
 
and
 
routed
 
to
 
the
 
authorized
 
credit
 
workflow.
 
KYC
 
&
 
KYB
 
Requirements
 
Customer/investor
 
KYC;
 
KYB
 
and
 
beneficial-owner
 
checks;
 
AML/sanctions
 
screening;
 
source-of-funds/source-of-wealth
 
checks
 
where
 
applicable;
 
risk
 
profiling;
 
suitability,
 
accreditation
 
or
 
eligibility
 
checks
 
where
 
required.
 
PERMITTED
 
Data
 
to
 
Collect
 
Application
 
and
 
eligibility
 
data,
 
credit
 
attributes,
 
investment-account
 
identifiers,
 
risk
 
profile,
 
approved
 
product
 
information,
 
order/transaction
 
references,
 
portfolio/service
 
data
 
and
 
required
 
consent/preferences.
 
RESTRICTED
 
/
 
PROHIBITED
 
Data
 
Passwords,
 
PINs,
 
OTPs,
 
full
 
payment
 
credentials,
 
unnecessary
 
credit-report
 
details,
 
confidential
 
trading
 
information
 
and
 
sensitive
 
financial
 
data
 
outside
 
the
 
approved
 
purpose.
 
Consent
 
Requirements
 
Use
 
purpose-specific
 
notice/consent
 
for
 
marketing
 
and
 
profiling
 
where
 
required;
 
capture
 
risk
 
and
 
product
 
disclosures;
 
record
 
customer
 
acknowledgements;
 
obtain
 
authorization
 
before
 
securities
 
orders,
 
investment
 
actions
 
or
 
loan
 
disbursements.
 
Guardrails
 
No
 
autonomous
 
investment
 
order,
 
credit
 
approval,
 
disbursement
 
or
 
suitability
 
conclusion
 
unless
 
the
 
configured
 
workflow
 
expressly
 
permits
 
it;
 
no
 
guaranteed-return
 
statements;
 
escalate
 
conflicts,
 
vulnerable
 
customers
 
and
 
unusual
 
transactions.
 
AI
 
/
 
Agent
 
Compliance
 
Rules
 
AI
 
must
 
distinguish
 
factual
 
information
 
from
 
regulated
 
advice;
 
use
 
approved
 
product
 
data;
 
avoid
 
guaranteed-return
 
or
 
approval
 
claims;
 
preserve
 
rationale
 
for
 
material
 
recommendations;
 
route
 
regulated
 
advice
 
and
 
decisions
 
to
 
qualified
 
staff
 
where
 
required.
 
Product
 
/
 
Module-Specific
 
Compliance

origination/servicing,
 
credit
 
decisioning,

investment,
 
portfolio
 
and
 
collections
 
modules
 
require

controls,
 
approval
 
workflows,
 
separation
 
of
 
duties
 
and
 
auditable
 
decision
 
records.

rules
